add_namespace = annex
country_event = { #Fate of Mongolia
	id = annex.7000
	title = annexation_321
	desc = annexation_321_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			determine_chinese_ally = yes
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_mongolia = no }
					remove_core_of = MON
				}
				else = {
					add_core_of = MON
				}
				remove_claim_by = MON
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_chinese_tag = yes
						tag = RUS
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			621 = { retain_state = yes } # IDFK
			611 = { retain_state = yes } # IDFK 
			612 = { retain_state = yes } # IDFK
			329 = { retain_state = yes } #Tuva
			1137 = { retain_state = yes } #Altai-Gobi
		}
	}

	option = { #Release Mongolia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7000 option annex.3013.a"
		name = annex.7000.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = MON }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			MON = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = MON_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			MON = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = MON_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to China
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annexation_events.7000 option annex.give_to_china"
		name = annex.give_to_china
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = china
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_major = yes
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:china }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:china = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:china }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					region = 152 #Outer Mongolia
					region = 244 #Inner Mongolia
				}
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7000 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_mongolia = yes }
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7000 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_mongolia = yes }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7000 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Gansu #NOT USED
	id = annex.7001
	title = annexation_decision_gansu
	desc = annexation_decision_gansu_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7001 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Sinkiang
	id = annex.7002
	title = annexation_617
	desc = annexation_617_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = { 
		hidden_effect = { 
			annexations_reset_central_asian_tags = yes
			determine_chinese_ally = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_chinese_tag = yes
						AND = {
							is_central_asian_tag = yes
							589 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Eastern Khazakstan
						}
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_chinese_tag = no
					589 = { #Eastern Khazakstan
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
							is_central_asian_tag = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:589.owner = { save_event_target_as = turkestan add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }
			}
			1099 = { retain_state = yes } #Aksai Chin
		}
	}

	option = { #Release Sinkiang
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7002 option annex.7002.a"
		name = annex.7002.a
		trigger = { 
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = ETR }
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_sinkiang = yes
				NOT = { is_core_of = var:tag_to_release }
			}
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			ETR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = ETR_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			ETR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = ETR_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #liberate united Central Asia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annexation_events.7002 option annex.asia.release_united_central_asia"
		name = annex.asia.release_united_central_asia
		trigger = {
			NOT = { country_exists = event_target:united_central_asia }
			is_central_asian_tag = no
			is_chinese_tag = no
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_event_target = china
				OR = {
					event_target:china = { is_russian_tag = no }
				}
				OR = {
					is_major = no
					is_ally_with = event_target:china
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_event_target = turkestan
				OR = {
					event_target:turkestan = { is_russian_tag = no }
				}
				OR = {
					is_major = no
					is_ally_with = event_target:turkestan
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SIK }
		annexations_release_united_central_asia = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Give it to Turkestan
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annexation_events.7002 option annex.give_to_turkestan"
		name = annex.give_to_turkestan
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = turkestan
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:turkestan }
				is_ally_with = event_target:china
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:turkestan = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:turkestan }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_sinkiang = yes
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give it to China
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annexation_events.7002 option annex.give_to_china"
		name = annex.give_to_china
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = china
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:china }
				is_ally_with = event_target:turkestan
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:china = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:china }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_sinkiang = yes
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7002 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_sinkiang = yes }
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7002 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_sinkiang = yes }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7002 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of East Turkestan / UNUSED
	id = annex.7003
	title = annexation_decision_east_turkestan
	desc = annexation_decision_east_turkestan_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					original_tag = RUS
					original_tag = ROC
					original_tag = CHI
					original_tag = SIK
					original_tag = KUM
					original_tag = XSM
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
	}

	option = { #Release East Turkestan
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7003 option annex.7003.a"
		name = annex.7003.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SIK
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = CHI
					tag = ROC
				}
				factor = 0
			}
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = RUS
				}
				factor = 50
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = ETR }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					NOT = {
						original_tag = RUS
					}
				}
			}
			ETR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = ETR_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					original_tag = RUS
				}
			}
			ETR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = ETR_Occupied_Monarchy_RUS
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
					NOT = {
						original_tag = ROC
					}
				}
			}
			ETR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = ETR_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
					original_tag = ROC
				}
			}
			ETR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = ETR_Occupied_Republic_ROC
			}
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7003 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		ETR = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7003 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = CHI
					tag = ROC
				}
				factor = 100
			}
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = RUS
				}
				factor = 50
			}
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SIK
				}
				factor = 100
			}
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7003 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Kumul
	id = annex.7004
	title = annexation_760
	desc = annexation_760_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate =  {
		hidden_effect = {
			determine_chinese_ally = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_chinese_tag = yes
						AND = {
							is_central_asian_tag = yes
							617 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Urumqi
						}
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					617 = { #Urumqi
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
							NOT = { tag = event_target:china }
						}
					}
				}
				var:617.owner = { save_event_target_as = sinkiang add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Release Kumul
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7004 option annex.7004.a"
		name = annex.7004.a
		trigger = { 
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = KUM }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			KUM = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = KUM_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			KUM = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = KUM_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give it to China
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annexation_events.7004 option annex.give_to_china"
		name = annex.give_to_china
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = china
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:china }
				is_ally_with = event_target:sinkiang
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:china = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:china }
		760 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes }
	}

	option = { #Give it to Sinkiang
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7004 option annex.7004.b"
		name = annex.7004.b
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = sinkiang
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:sinkiang }
				is_ally_with = event_target:china
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:sinkiang = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:sinkiang }
		760 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes }
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7004 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		760 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes }

			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}

	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7004 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = {

country_event = { #Fate of Xibei San Man
	id = annex.7005
	title = annexation_754
	desc = annexation_754_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = { 
			604 = { retain_state = yes }
			755 = { retain_state = yes }
			determine_chinese_ally = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_chinese_tag = yes
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					759 = { #Kunlun
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
							NOT = { tag = event_target:china}
						}
					}
				}
				var:759.owner = { save_event_target_as = sinkiang add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Release Xibei San Ma
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7005 option annex.7005.a"
		name = annex.7005.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}

			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = XSM }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			XSM = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = XSM_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			XSM = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = XSM_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:china }
				is_ally_with = event_target:sinkiang
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:china = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:china }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_xibei_san_ma = yes
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give it to Sinkiang
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7005 option annex.7005.b"
		name = annex.7005.b
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = sinkiang
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:sinkiang }
				is_ally_with = event_target:china
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:sinkiang = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:sinkiang }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_xibei_san_ma = yes
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord

country_event = { #Fate of Tibet
	id = annex.7006
	title = annexation_757
	desc = annexation_757_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = { 
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_tibet = no }
					remove_core_of = TIB
				}
				else = {
					add_core_of = TIB
				}
				remove_claim_by = TIB
			}
			determine_chinese_ally = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_chinese_tag = yes
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			601 = { retain_state = yes }
			752 = { retain_state = yes }
			434 = { retain_state = yes }
			604 = { retain_state = yes }
		}
	}

	option = { #Release Tibet
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7006 option annex.7006.a"
		name = annex.7006.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = CHI
					original_tag = ROC
				}
				factor = 20
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = TIB }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			TIB = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = TIB_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			TIB = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = TIB_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Give it to China
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annexation_events.7006 option annex.give_to_china"
		name = annex.give_to_china
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = china
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_ally_with = event_target:china
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:china = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:china }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_tibet = yes
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7006 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_tibet = yes }
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7006 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_core_of = TIB }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7006 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

		trigger = {
			country_exists = BFE
			BFE = {
				is_subject = yes
			}
		}

		every_state = {
			limit = { 
				is_indochina = yes
			} 
			add_core_of = VIN
		}
		VIN = {
			set_cosmetic_tag = VIN_Indochinese_Republic
			save_global_event_target_as = INC_TAG
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = VIN }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_subject = CAM }
			annex_country = { target = CAM }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_subject = LAO }
			annex_country = { target = LAO }
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7015 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		VIN = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7015 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
			original_tag = BFE
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_vietnam = yes }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7015 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Laos
	id = annex.7016
	title = annexation_670
	desc = annexation_670_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_laos = no }
					remove_core_of = LAO
				}
				else = {
					add_core_of = LAO
				}
				remove_claim_by = LAO
			}
			if = { 
				limit = { 
					OR = { 
						has_cosmetic_tag = VIN_Indochinese_Republic
						original_tag = SIA
						original_tag = BFE
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = { 
					VIN = { 
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes 
						has_cosmetic_tag = VIN_Indochinese_Republic
					} 
				}
				VIN = { save_event_target_as = indochina add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { 
					SIA = { 
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes 
					} 
				}
				SIA = { save_event_target_as = siam add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { 
					BFE = { 
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes 
					} 
				}
				BFE = { save_event_target_as = british_far_east add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }
			}
			1122 = { retain_state = yes }
		}
	}

	option = { #Release Laos
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7016 option annex.7016.a"
		name = annex.7016.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				annexations_can_annex = yes
				factor = 0
			}
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					has_subject = BFE
				}
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = LAO }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			LAO = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = LAO_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			LAO = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = LAO_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Leave it to Siam
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7016 option annex.7016.b"
		name = annex.give_to_siam
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = siam
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:siam }
				OR = { 
					is_ally_with = event_target:british_far_east
					is_ally_with = event_target:indochina
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_ally_with = event_target:siam
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:siam = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:siam }
		CAM = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Leave it to British Far East

		name = annex.give_to_bfe
		trigger = {

		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:british_far_east }
				OR = { 
					is_ally_with = event_target:siam
					is_ally_with = event_target:indochina
				}
			}
			modifier = {

				is_ally_with = event_target:british_far_east

			limit = { is_malaya = yes }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7019 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }

country_event = { #Fate of Japan
	id = annex.7020
	title = annexation_282
	desc = annexation_282_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = { 
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_japan = yes 
					}
					add_core_of = JAP
				}
				else = {
					remove_core_of = JAP
					remove_claim_by = JAP
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_japan_unifier_tag = yes
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			JAP = { 
				remove_ideas = PB_JAP_Republican_Terror_Legacy
				remove_ideas = PB_JAP_Sakurakai
				remove_ideas = PB_JAP_Reactionary_Plot
			}
			536 = { retain_state = yes }
			1136 = { retain_state = yes }
			annexations_create_japan_event_targets = yes 
			if = {
				limit = { annexations_germany_is_independent_ally_or_puppet_of_ROOT = no }
				if = {
					limit = {
						528 = { # South Japan
							annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
							owner = {
								NOT = { tag = event_target:japan }
								ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
							}
						}
					}
					var:528.owner = { save_event_target_as = south_japan add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						1135 = { # North Japan
							annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
							owner = {
								NOT = { tag = event_target:japan }
								NOT = { tag = event_target:south_japan }
								ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
							}
						}
					}
					var:1135.owner = { save_event_target_as = north_japan add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Release Japanese Empire
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7020 option annex.7020.a"
		name = annex.7020.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = JAP }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		if = { 
			limit = { ROOT = { has_custom_puppet_JAP = yes  } } 
			setup_custom_puppet_JAP = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = has_custom_puppet
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			JAP = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = JAP_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			JAP = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = JAP_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Give to Japan Tag 1
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annexation_events.7020 option annex.give_to_japan"
		name = annex.give_to_japan
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = japan
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_japan = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:japan }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:north_japan
					is_ally_with = event_target:south_japan
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:japan = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:japan }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_japan = yes
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to Japan tag 3 (North)
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annexation_events.7020 option annexation_events.7020.b"
		name = annexation_events.7020.b
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = north_japan
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 75
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_japan = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:north_japan }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:japan
					is_ally_with = event_target:south_japan
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:north_japan = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:north_japan }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_japan = yes
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to Japan tag 2 (South)
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annexation_events.7020 option annexation_events.7020.c"
		name = annexation_events.7020.c
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = south_japan
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 75
			modifier = {

			}

				}
			}

			}
		}

	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7020 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }

country_event = { #Fate of Siberia
	id = annex.7023
	title = annexation_571
	desc = annexation_571_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = { 
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						original_tag = RUS
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			every_state = {
				remove_claim_by = SIB
				if = {
					limit = { is_siberia = yes }
					add_core_of = SIB
				}
				else = {
					remove_core_of = SIB
				}
			}
			40 = { retain_state = yes }
			329 = { retain_state = yes }
			563 = { retain_state = yes }
			564 = { retain_state = yes }
			566 = { retain_state = yes }
			565 = { retain_state = yes }
			574 = { retain_state = yes }
			575 = { retain_state = yes }
			644 = { retain_state = yes }
			408 = { retain_state = yes }
			409 = { retain_state = yes }
			560 = { retain_state = yes }
			657 = { retain_state = yes }
			561 = { retain_state = yes }
			562 = { retain_state = yes }
			1084 = { retain_state = yes }
			1085 = { retain_state = yes }
			637 = { retain_state = yes }
		}
	}

	option = { #Release an independent Siberia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7023 option annex.7023.a"
		name = annex.7023.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_russia = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = {
					is_ally_with = RUS
					RUS = { is_subject = no }
					annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_russia = no
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SIB }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			SIB = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SIB_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			SIB = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SIB_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Reintegrate Siberia into Russia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7023 option annex.7023.b"
		name = annex.7023.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			RUS = {
				exists = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_russia = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 90
				AND = {
					is_ally_with = RUS
					RUS = { is_subject = no }
					annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_russia = no
				}
			}
		}
		
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = RUS }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_siberia = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7023 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_siberia = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7023 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_siberia = yes
			}
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7023 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = { 
		recheck_annexations = yes 
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Kazakhstan
	id = annex.7024
	title = annexation_402
	desc = annexation_402_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = { 
			annexations_reset_central_asian_tags = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_state_of = {
						target = { 571 407 } #Omsk, Tashkent
						is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					571 = { #Omsk
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
					}
				}
				var:571.owner = { save_event_target_as = russia add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					1394 = { #Kyzylorda
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = event_target:russia }
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:1394.owner = { save_event_target_as = turkestan add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #liberate united Central Asia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annexation_events.7025 option annex.asia.release_united_central_asia"
		name = annex.asia.release_united_central_asia
		trigger = {
			NOT = { country_exists = event_target:united_central_asia }
			is_central_asian_tag = no
			is_chinese_tag = no
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_major = no
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_ally_with = event_target:russia
				OR = {
					event_target:russia = { is_russian_tag = no }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_ally_with = event_target:turkestan
				OR = {
					event_target:turkestan = { is_russian_tag = no }
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = KAZ }
		annexations_release_united_central_asia = yes
	}

	option = { #Release an independent Kazakhstan
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7024 option annex.7024.a"
		name = annex.7024.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = KAZ }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			KAZ = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = KAZ_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			KAZ = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = KAZ_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	
	option = { #give to Omsk
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annexation_events.7024 option annexation_events.asia.give_to_russia"
		name = annexation_events.asia.give_to_russia
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = russia
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				event_target:russia = { is_russian_tag = yes }
				is_germany_or_splinter_tag = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:russia }
				is_ally_with = event_target:turkestan
				OR = {
					event_target:russia = { is_russian_tag = no }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:russia = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:russia }
		KAZ = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #give to Turkestan
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annexation_events.7024 option annex.give_to_turkestan"
		name = annex.give_to_turkestan
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = turkestan
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				event_target:turkestan = { is_russian_tag = yes }
				is_germany_or_splinter_tag = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:turkestan }
				is_ally_with = event_target:russia
				OR = {
					event_target:turkestan = { is_russian_tag = no }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:turkestan = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:turkestan }
		KAZ = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7024 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_alash = yes }
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7024 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_alash = yes }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7024 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	after = { 
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

				is_germany_or_splinter_tag = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:stalinobad }
				is_ally_with = event_target:turkemistan
				OR = {
					event_target:stalinobad = { is_russian_tag = no }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:stalinobad = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:stalinobad }
		BUK = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7026 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }

			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}

country_event = { #Fate of TURKMENISTAN
	id = annex.7027
	title = annexation_584
	desc = annexation_584_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = { 
			annexations_reset_central_asian_tags = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_state_of = {
						target = { 405 587 416 } #Atyrau, West Khazakstan, Khorasan
						is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					405 = { #Atyrau
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
					}
				}
				var:405.owner = { save_event_target_as = bukhara add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					587 = { #Khazakstan
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = event_target:bukhara }
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:587.owner = { save_event_target_as = alash add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					416 = { #Khorasan
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = event_target:bukhara }
							NOT = { tag = event_target:alash }
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:416.owner = { save_event_target_as = persia add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #liberate united Central Asia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annexation_events.7027 option annex.asia.release_united_central_asia"

			NOT = { country_exists = event_target:united_central_asia }
			is_central_asian_tag = no
			is_chinese_tag = no
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_major = no
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_ally_with = event_target:bukhara
				OR = {
					event_target:bukhara = { is_russian_tag = no }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_ally_with = event_target:alash
				OR = {
					event_target:alash = { is_russian_tag = no }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_ally_with = event_target:persia
				OR = {
					event_target:persia = { is_russian_tag = no }
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = KOK }
		annexations_release_united_central_asia = yes
	}

		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7031 option annex.7031.a"
		name = annex.7031.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 60
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:omsk }
				annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_russia = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_ally_with = event_target:omsk
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = FER }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			FER = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = FER_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			FER = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = FER_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Incorporate it into Omsk
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7031 option annex.7031.a"
		name = annex.give_to_omsk
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = omsk
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				is_ally_with = event_target:omsk
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:omsk }
				annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_russia = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:omsk = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:omsk }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_russian_far_east = yes
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7031 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_russian_far_east = yes
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7031 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_russian_far_east = yes
			}
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7031 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

		hidden_effect = { 
			1148 = { retain_state = yes } #Sabah

			1352 = { retain_state = yes } #Zamboanga
			1579 = { retain_state = yes } #Sulu
			every_owned_state = {
				if = {

						is_philippines = yes

					remove_core_of = PHI
					remove_claim_by = PHI
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						original_tag = DAS
					}

					}
				}

				remove_ideas = { 
					PB_Idea_PRA_1

			}
		}

		}

			}

					has_monarchist_government = yes

				set_cosmetic_tag = PHI_Occupied_Monarchy

	}

	option = { #Release Philippines Whole
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7036 option annex.7036.a"
		name = annex.7036.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}

				factor = 0
			}
		}

		1352 = { clear_retain_state = yes } #Zamboanga
		1579 = { clear_retain_state = yes } #Sulu

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = PHI }

                    owner = {
                        NOT = { tag = event_target:dutch_east_indies }
                        owns_state = 669 #Northern Papua
                        ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
                    }
                }
            }
            var:737.owner = { save_event_target_as = german_pacific }
        }
	}

	option = { # Release an independent Papua
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7044 option annex.7044.a"
		name = annex.7044.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}

			base = 5
		}

           	has_event_target = german_pacific

            base = 70

                factor = 0
                annexations_can_annex = yes
            }

                factor = 0
                NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:german_pacific }
                is_ally_with = event_target:dutch_east_indies
                #annexations_AI_will_consider_returning_colonies = yes
            }

                factor = 0.1

            }

						}
					}

				factor = 0

			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = AFG }

					is_ally_with = event_target:sind
					is_ally_with = event_target:balochistan

				factor = 0.1

		}

			base = 40
			modifier = {

					original_tag = INS
					original_tag = USS
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		if = {
			limit = { INS = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
			INS = { save_event_target_as = dutch_east_indies }
		}
	}

	option = { # Release Java Whole
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7059 option annex.7059.a"
		name = annex.7059.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:dutch_east_indies = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}

		if = { 
			limit = { ROOT = { has_custom_puppet_USS = yes  } } 
			setup_custom_puppet_USS = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = has_custom_puppet
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			USS = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = USS_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			USS = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = USS_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = USS }
		335 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #West Java
		1338 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #Banten
		1339 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #East Java
		1147 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } 
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #give to INS
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7059 option annex.7059.b"
		name = annex.7059.b
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = dutch_east_indies
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				has_subject = event_target:dutch_east_indies
				annexations_can_annex = no
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				is_ally_with = event_target:dutch_east_indies
				annexations_can_annex = no
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:dutch_east_indies }
		335 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } 
		1338 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } 
		1339 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } 
		1147 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } 
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7059 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		335 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } 
		1338 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } 
		1339 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } 
		1147 = { transfer_if_owned = yes }
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7059 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		335 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } 
		1338 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } 
		1339 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } 
		1147 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes }
	}

	option = { # Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7059 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
        recheck_annexations = yes
    }
}

		}

			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = AWD }

		if = {

				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			AWD = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = AWD_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

				}
			}

	id = annex.7082
	title = annexation_854
	desc = annexation_854_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = { 
		hidden_effect = { 
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_indian_unifier = yes
						852 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #
						428 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			determine_indian_ally = yes
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					annexations_india_is_puppet_or_independent_ally_of_ROOT = no
					852 = { #sanghli
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = event_target:india }
							NOT = { tag = event_target:india_two }
							NOT = { tag = event_target:india_three }
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:852.owner = { save_event_target_as = hyderabad add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					annexations_india_is_puppet_or_independent_ally_of_ROOT = no
					428 = { #gujarat
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = event_target:india }
							NOT = { tag = event_target:india_two }
							NOT = { tag = event_target:india_three }
							NOT = { tag = event_target:hyderabad }
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:428.owner = { save_event_target_as = gujarat add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Leave Surate to Hyderabad
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7082 option annex.7082.a"
		name = annex.7082.a
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = hyderabad
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:hyderabad }
				OR = { 
					is_ally_with = event_target:india
					is_ally_with = event_target:india_two
					is_ally_with = event_target:india_three
					is_ally_with = event_target:gujarat
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:hyderabad = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:hyderabad }
		854 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes }
	}
	
	option = { #Grant it to Gujarat
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7082 option annex.7082.b"
		name = annex.7082.b
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = gujarat
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:gujarat }
				OR = { 
					is_ally_with = event_target:india
					is_ally_with = event_target:india_two
					is_ally_with = event_target:india_three
					is_ally_with = event_target:hyderabad
				}
			}
			modifier = {

			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:gujarat }
		854 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes }
	}
	
	option = { #Give to india
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annexation_events.7080 option annex.give_to_india"
		name = annex.give_to_india
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = india
		}

			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0

				OR = {

					is_ally_with = event_target:india_three
					is_ally_with = event_target:hyderabad
					is_ally_with = event_target:gujarat

country_event = { # Fate of Burma
	id = annex.7088
	title = annexation_288
	desc = annexation_288_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_owned_state = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_burma = yes
					}
					add_core_of = BUR
				}
				else = { 
					remove_core_of = BUR
					remove_claim_by = BUR
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					#is_indian_unifier_tag = yes
					any_state_of = {
						target = { 432 1109 } #Assam, Manipur

				limit = {
					var:1109.owner = { #Manipur

						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
				var:1109.owner = { save_event_target_as = india add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					var:432.owner = { #Assam
						is_indian_unifier = yes
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
				var:432.owner = { save_event_target_as = india add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }

				}
			}

				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = BUR_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

				limit = {
					1416 = { #nakhon
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = event_target:laos }
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:1416.owner = { save_event_target_as = nakhon add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Leave it to nakhon
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7103 option annex.7103.a"
		name = annex.7103.a
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = nakhon
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:nakhon }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:laos
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:nakhon = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:nakhon }
		1122 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #
	}
	
	option = { #Give it to laos
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7103 option annex.7103.b"
		name = annex.7103.b
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = laos
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:laos }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:nakhon
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:laos = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:laos }
		1122 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7101 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		1122 = { transfer_if_owned = yes }
	}

	option = { #Integrate

		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		1122 = {
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { # Cancel

		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
        recheck_annexations = yes
    }
}

country_event = { # Fate of Pattani
	id = annex.7103
	title = annexation_decision_1125
	desc = annexation_decision_1125_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = { 
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						724 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Krai
						1141 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Perak
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					724 = { #Krai
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:724.owner = { save_event_target_as = siam add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					1141 = { #Perak
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = event_target:siam }
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:1141.owner = { save_event_target_as = malaya add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Leave it to siam
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7103 option annex.7103.a"
		name = annex.7103.a
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = siam
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:siam }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:malaya
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:siam = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:siam }
		1125 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #
		1140 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #
		1142 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #
	}
	
	option = { #Give it to Malaya
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7103 option annex.7103.b"
		name = annex.7103.b
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = malaya
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:malaya }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:siam
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:malaya = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:siam }
		1125 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #
		1140 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #
		1142 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7103 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		1125 = { transfer_if_owned = yes }
		1140 = { transfer_if_owned = yes }
		1142 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } 
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7103 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		1125 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes }
		1140 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes }
		1142 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } 
	}

	option = { # Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7103 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
        recheck_annexations = yes
    }
}

country_event = { # Fate of Singapore
	id = annex.7104
	title = annexation_decision_916
	desc = annexation_decision_916_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = { #Release Singapore
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7104 option annex.7104.a"
		name = annex.7104.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			SIA = {
				exists = yes
			}
			ROOT = {
				NOT = {
					tag = SIA
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SNG }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			SIA = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SNG_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			SIA = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SNG_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Give it to Malaya
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7104 option annex.7104.b"
		name = annex.7104.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			MAL = {
				exists = yes
			}
			ROOT = {
				NOT = {
					tag = MAL
				}
			}

			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		}

			}

		}

	}

				annexations_can_annex = yes

			}

			}

			}
			modifier = {

			}

					original_tag = CHI

				}
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		CHI = {
			transfer_state = 622
		}
		622 = { add_claim_by = CHI }
	}
	
	option = { #Leave it to the Republic of China
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7110 option annex.7110.b"
		name = annex.7110.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			ROC = {
				exists = yes
			}
			ROOT = {
				NOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					original_tag = CHI
					original_tag = ROC
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		ROC = {
			transfer_state = 622
		}
		622 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
	}
	
	option = { #Release Shanxi
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7110 option annex.7110.c"
		name = annex.7110.c
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = {
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = CHI
					original_tag = ROC
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = RUS
					original_tag = ENG
				}
				factor = 60
			}
		}
		
		622 = { add_core_of = SHX }

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SHX }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			SHX = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SHX_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			SHX = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SHX_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7110 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		SHX = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7110 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				annexations_can_annex = yes
				original_tag = CHI
				original_tag = ROC
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				ROOT = {
					OR = {
						original_tag = CHI
						original_tag = ROC
					}
				}
			}
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_core_of = SHX }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { # Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7110 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
        recheck_annexations = yes
    }
}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:madurai }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:madurai = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:madurai }
		422 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Ceylon
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7119 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		422 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Ceylon
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7119 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		422 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } #Ceylon
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7119 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}
